- **re-il2cpp-static-triage**: First-pass static triage of a Unity IL2CPP game. Use when the user says "what classes does this Unity game have", "give me the C# class graph", "what's in Assembly-CSharp.dll", or hands you a Unity 2019-2022 game directory. Calls re-il2cpp.get_images + re-il2cpp.get_assembly_types + re-il2cpp.search_strings and produces a one-page class graph report. Explicitly does NOT crack protection on GameAssembly.dll or read function bodies — for those, escalate to re-drm-fingerprint and re-decompile. ([Heretek-AI/heretek-claude-harness](https://github.com/Heretek-AI/heretek-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/plugins/re-ai/skills/re-il2cpp-static-triage))
